# Child pedal-powered toy tricycle: supplied-component assembly and first whole release

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This method covers a new complete non-electric child pedal-powered toy tricycle with a welded tubular-metal frame and coated fork/handlebar module, direct front-wheel pedals with a solid one-piece steel crank, solid rubber tyres on spokeless wheels, a rubber seat, actual version-specific wheel bearings and steering bushes, grips, stops and all configured fastenings. The bounded foreground route receives complete finished coated frame and fork modules and actual supplied wheel/seat/axle articles, checks incoming compatibility, mechanically fits and tightens them, performs actual configured complete-toy inspection/testing and records the first whole quality release. Supplier fabrication, surface preparation, coating and component manufacture must remain in compatible upstream inventories. It is not a purchase of the finished toy followed by packaging. On-site metal forming/welding/coating or injection moulding is outside this selected assembly route; if it actually occurs, collect its real raw materials, each preparation, energy, species and losses and obtain applicable route evidence before extending this same record's dataset scope. No omission or zero may conceal it.

Exclude adult, passenger/cargo or road-transport tricycles; bicycles and balance bikes; scooters; electric or combustion toys; hand-lever and solely push-propelled ride-ons; dolls' carriages and strollers; multi-rider/recumbent variants; toy train/model/construction sets; loose wheels, raw steel/plastic, component-only kits and remanufacturing as the reference terminal. Riding, child metabolism, road infrastructure, use-stage repairs and end of life are not part of the manufacturing reference. Actual pre-dispatch storage/handling/packing and actual returned-product work within the declared service horizon remain separately traceable to original release cohorts.

This is a qualitative technical synthesis, not a same-SKU or same-factory bill of materials. Winther's full manufacturer-authored catalogue (distributed PDF), product/component illustrations and 451 assembly/spares originals positively establish the toy terminal and real module structure; the catalogue also illustrates upstream welding/coating. The old manual's nylon-bearing wording does not override a current drawing or the catalogue's ball-bearing version. KRIDDO explicitly describes tricycle component compatibility and pre-exit safety checks; its adjacent balance-bike welding discussion is not proof of this toy's own fabrication route. None of these documents supplies universal alloy grade, polymer fractions, paint chemistry/thickness, temperature, pressure, duration, factory energy, load/age/test limit, certification, lifespan or nominal single-toy mass for this PCR. An actual configured release plan and supply completeness must be collected.

| reference_flow_link | finished |

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |

| Reference flow property | Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` |
| Reference unit group | Units of mass `93a60a57-a4c8-11da-a746-0800200c9a66` |
| Reference unit | kg |
| Required qualifiers | actual manufacturer and model; serial and first-release cohort; child-toy purpose and direct front-wheel pedal drive; frame substrate grade and welded geometry; actual coating and supplied frame completeness; fork and handlebar module version; seat rubber specification and fitting; solid tyre and hub specification; steel crank and non-slip pedal configuration; wheel bearing and head-tube bush identities by version; grips and steering-stop configuration; axle fasteners and actual component supply overlaps; all integral delivered accessories; calibrated net whole-toy weighing and tare; configuration-specific acceptance plan and results; actual rework permission and postwork disposition; site geography and production period; utility state and upstream supplier coverage; opening and closing raw WIP and unassigned-store vectors; released FG and original-cohort service horizon; gross dispatch returns and original-dispatch reversal; each packaging pool and supplier-included ownership; allocation evidence and unresolved actual quantities |

Every required qualifier belongs in the real dataset metadata, process notes, reference-flow comment or equivalent field. Native broad whole-family use is restricted by §1 and by the complete terminal conditions on the finished card. Q is measured whole net mass; no source catalogue single-item mass is adopted.

Q0 is an acceptance-event account: sum independently weighed complete net masses of each unique first-accepted configuration once, locking that original mass to its serial/cohort. Q=Q0 for manufacture; original Qc=Q0 for later observed service. Partition every originally accepted identity into disjoint net remaining deliveries, all actual on-site accepted states (stored, held, damaged, sampled, returned, repair WIP), irreversible whole-product losses and other terminal dispositions on its original acceptance-mass basis. Record every state/exit; delivery-plus-closing-FG is only a simplified bridge when other postgate states/exits are proven absent. Returns, repeated tests, repairs, dispatch loops, added parts and changed moisture/current mass create no new Q0. Partial material/water loss is separately measured physical change; do not restore that loss into the surviving product and also add it as an outlet. Current physical mass/stock balances are independent of the original event-mass partition. Original service denominator remains even if nothing survives; zero or unknown Q0 retains raw loads and no unit result. Actual item count is an identity reconciliation, never a default kg-per-toy conversion.

The complete-product route and physical component relationships are positive evidence; the assembly manual is adult/user assembly and does not establish a fully assembled factory shipment or an actual external-supplier purchase by Winther. Treat supplied-module manufacture as a bounded technical synthesis supported by the real modules and independent factory component/check evidence. A concrete dataset must identify each actual complete producer, its real raw-material preparation/fabrication/welding/surface treatment/coating/moulding/energy/species/waste gate and delivered composition, including any controlled on-site producer. Link that complete producer inventory once or expand its actual operations mutually exclusively. An invoice, an identity UUID, a user manual, a spare-part drawing or a promised future SOP alone never supplies those burdens. If work is actually done at the assembly site, include the complete real process before use; merely renaming it upstream does not exclude it. Missing essential core production/matrix procedure remains an explicit hold, not a condition silently waived by a software check. No shared-factory/SKU condition or empirical recipe is invented.

Sum measured accepted net masses to Q; no assumed item-to-kg factor. |

Integrate only actual rates; convert raw units and physical state first from independent measurements. Total integrated volume is not multiplied by duration again. No universal Mass/kg constraint applies to all rows. |

Determine a numerator and its actual cohort denominator before the unique single_quotient operation in §8. Cards and protocol aggregation retrieve the same saved normalized_exchange; neither divides again. Zero/missing denominator preserves actual loads/stocks and yields no unit result. |

Hazard, real treatment, composition and uncertain assignment remain explicit. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

| b_inventory_service_vapour_out | `service_vapour_out` | Actual independently measured H2O vapour loss during storage, handling, cleanup or actual operation. Do not force an equal opposite input, treat all water as evaporated or fit two unknowns to one mass residual. An integrated vapour quantity is not multiplied by time again. Constituent water already leaving in a wet article or liquid effluent is excluded from this same-direction vapour term. Native Elementary flow is H2O water vapour, CAS7732-18-5, emissions to air with unspecified air subcompartment, Mass/kg. The synonym dump contains liquid water, ice and isotopic-water strings; these do not override the vapour basename, CAS and air destination. Use only measured ordinary H2O vapour leaving this foreground; not liquid wastewater, humidity uptake, steam purchased as Product, heavy/tritiated water or an aggregate VOC/COD indicator. Match a more specific real air compartment when known. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| b_inventory_carton_p | `carton_p` | Conditional separately purchased new article P for this one corrugated shipping carton. The single-quotient numerator is the complete actual gross physical leg V, before economic shares or stock/burden correction. This disclosure row creates no second purchase, provider burden or credit. P and I are mutually exclusive ownership tags for the same inbound article; a whole wet incoming article includes its constituent water once. Record real influx even when the article/water remains in closing stock. All opening/closing empty, attached-WIP/FG and reusable-pool quantities stay in the pool equation. Only other same-direction incoming/outgoing flow terms can create X deduplication; a stock or opposite row cannot. Internal reuse cancels. Trace its one original upstream owner and any valid burden handover in the separate burden dimension; full physical gross V is never reduced by an allocation share. One actual complete corrugated shipping carton, including its supplied board, adhesive, print and water once. Declare actual fibre/board construction, coatings, dimensions, wet tare, provider gate and composition independently. It cannot represent a liner, stretch film, tape, wooden article, all packaging or waste. A generic unspecified packaging family does not establish this specific whole article; retain its unbound identity until a compatible complete native record is verified. | `winther-451-assembly`; `winther-451-spares`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

| b_full_collection_context | all collection records | The following protocols collect actual unnormalized event/cohort records. Their aggregation_rule names the final reference basis only: it retrieves the one saved normalized_exchange calculated after reconciliation and allocation, and does not divide raw values. The gross view and assigned LCI burden view are separate non-additive views. Actual additional atomic flows require their own row and protocol before a dataset is usable. No site data, missing occurrence or missing UUID is filled with zero. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| b_full_reference_context | complete reference and producer conditions | Every required qualifier belongs in the real dataset metadata, process notes, reference-flow comment or equivalent field. Native broad whole-family use is restricted by §1 and by the complete terminal conditions on the finished card. Q is measured whole net mass; no source catalogue single-item mass is adopted. Q0 is an acceptance-event account: sum independently weighed complete net masses of each unique first-accepted configuration once, locking that original mass to its serial/cohort. Q=Q0 for manufacture; original Qc=Q0 for later observed service. Partition every originally accepted identity into disjoint net remaining deliveries, all actual on-site accepted states (stored, held, damaged, sampled, returned, repair WIP), irreversible whole-product losses and other terminal dispositions on its original acceptance-mass basis. Record every state/exit; delivery-plus-closing-FG is only a simplified bridge when other postgate states/exits are proven absent. Returns, repeated tests, repairs, dispatch loops, added parts and changed moisture/current mass create no new Q0. Partial material/water loss is separately measured physical change; do not restore that loss into the surviving product and also add it as an outlet. Current physical mass/stock balances are independent of the original event-mass partition. Original service denominator remains even if nothing survives; zero or unknown Q0 retains raw loads and no unit result. Actual item count is an identity reconciliation, never a default kg-per-toy conversion. The complete-product route and physical component relationships are positive evidence; the assembly manual is adult/user assembly and does not establish a fully assembled factory shipment or an actual external-supplier purchase by Winther. Treat supplied-module manufacture as a bounded technical synthesis supported by the real modules and independent factory component/check evidence. A concrete dataset must identify each actual complete producer, its real raw-material preparation/fabrication/welding/surface treatment/coating/moulding/energy/species/waste gate and delivered composition, including any controlled on-site producer. Link that complete producer inventory once or expand its actual operations mutually exclusively. An invoice, an identity UUID, a user manual, a spare-part drawing or a promised future SOP alone never supplies those burdens. If work is actually done at the assembly site, include the complete real process before use; merely renaming it upstream does not exclude it. Missing essential core production/matrix procedure remains an explicit hold, not a condition silently waived by a software check. No shared-factory/SKU condition or empirical recipe is invented. | `winther-toy-catalogue-2025`; `winther-451-assembly`; `winther-451-spares`; `kriddo-factory-assembly-checks`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| b_classification_context | child riding toy scope | Official CPC3.0 notes explicitly include children tricycles in38510 and exclude bicycles49921; dolls/parts and scale-model categories have distinct neighbours. Use only as classification context, alongside the independently evidenced real complete child-ridden tricycle, native whole-product hardfields and this bounded route. No doll-carriage, real-infant pram, scale toy, bicycle, scooter sport product, component, transport service or whole-leaf/mapping approval is inferred. | `un-cpc3-toys`; `winther-toy-catalogue-2025`; `winther-451-spares` |

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| assembly | Incoming components and mechanical assembly | required | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | Original first-release mass Q or Qc; physical V retained separately |
| release | Complete-toy inspection and first release | required | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | finished |
| service | Original-cohort warehouse and actual post-release work | conditional | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | Original first-release mass Q or Qc; physical V retained separately |
| packaging | Individual gross packaging pools | conditional | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | Original first-release mass Q or Qc; physical V retained separately |
| assets | Durable tools and actual repair/fluid stocks | conditional | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | Original first-release mass Q or Qc; physical V retained separately |
| treatment | Once-owned actual waste treatment | conditional | Actual occurrence with full measurement and ownership records | Manufacturing or separate original-cohort accounting dimension; no second Q | Original first-release mass Q or Qc; physical V retained separately |

### Process: Incoming components and mechanical assembly (`assembly`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Finished powder-coated welded tubular-metal toy-tricycle frame (`frame`)

Whole supplied frame includes welded seat supports and rear structure only as listed in the actual supply BOM. Verify substrate alloy/grade and coating state; the primary sources do not fix an alloy or universal grade. Exclude the separately accounted fork, wheels, axle and seat. This receiving site performs mechanical assembly; upstream fabrication, surface preparation and coating remain in the complete compatible frame provider.

- Selected flow: Finished powder-coated welded tubular-metal toy-tricycle frame
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg
- Amount rule: Retrieve the saved normalized_exchange for cp_frame; raw collection and this retrieval perform no extra division.
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per 1 kg reference flow
- Basis kind: Reference flow (`reference_flow`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_frame`
- Sources: `winther-toy-catalogue-2025`; `winther-451-spares`

###### Finished coated toy-tricycle fork and handlebar assembly (`fork`)

One actual finished fork/handlebar module. Record included grips, nylon head-tube bushes, rubber stops, pivot fastener and fitting kit individually in its non-purchased composition subledger. Only separately received items use their separate purchase rows. The 451 spare drawing shows bundled fitments; never add the same fitment twice.

- Selected flow: Finished coated toy-tricycle fork and handlebar assembly
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg
- Amount rule: Retrieve the saved normalized_exchange for cp_fork; raw collection and this retrieval perform no extra division.
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per 1 kg reference flow
- Basis kind: Reference flow (`reference_flow`)

##### Waste flows

###### Actual separate whole contaminated wipe from assembly (`assembly_contaminated_wipe`)

Only a real separately collected complete contaminated cotton wipe outlet. Qt includes substrate, retained grease/cleaner/particles and water once; independently measured source components are nested subaccounts, not separate exported wastes. Preserve the actual full wet transfer and receiver demand independently of attributed per-cohort A_j/Q0. If it enters the one actual mixed_manufacturing_waste outlet, keep only its nonadditive origin ledger and do not also use this separate export or treatment. If several cohorts contribute to one event, retain the whole actual event once and distinct nonoverlapping attributable vectors, never the whole Qt charged anew to each cohort. Hazard, real treatment, composition and uncertain assignment remain explicit.

- Selected flow: Whole contaminated cotton wipe waste from assembly
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg

###### Actual collected cleaning wastewater (`assembly_wastewater`)

Conditional one real liquid wastewater outlet with water, actual dissolved/suspended species and contaminants measured. Do not borrow an electrolytic-manganese washing wastewater UUID or an unverified generic wastewater type. COD, BOD, total nitrogen, VOC and total dust are indicators, not specific molecular masses. Match actual treatment and avoid re-adding its water as a second liquid output.

- Selected flow: Actual collected cleaning wastewater
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg

###### One actual mixed manufacturing-waste outlet (`mixed_manufacturing_waste`)

Conditionally owns the one real mixed outlet including measured failed parts, samples, dirty wipes and any packaging origins actually co-collected. Keep origin masses independently measured and nonoverlapping. Failed, defective, destructively tested and sold manufacturing residues retain manufacturing and treatment burdens; neither external transfer nor revenue alone removes them.

Native Waste flow is a global at-plant production mix from final assembly and factory testing, Mass/kg; no quantified amount, treatment route or credit is specified. Use only for one actually collected mixed manufacturing-waste outlet. Identify constituents and origins in a disjoint subledger; do not add that subledger or its packaging W components to this outlet total again. Segregated outputs get their own rows and cannot also be inside this total. Hazard and provider compatibility require actual evidence.

##### Elementary flows

###### Ordinary H2O vapour released to air (`assembly_vapour_out`)

Actual independently measured H2O vapour loss during storage, handling, cleanup or actual operation. Do not force an equal opposite input, treat all water as evaporated or fit two unknowns to one mass residual. An integrated vapour quantity is not multiplied by time again. Constituent water already leaving in a wet article or liquid effluent is excluded from this same-direction vapour term.

Native Elementary flow is H2O water vapour, CAS7732-18-5, emissions to air with unspecified air subcompartment, Mass/kg. The synonym dump contains liquid water, ice and isotopic-water strings; these do not override the vapour basename, CAS and air destination. Use only measured ordinary H2O vapour leaving this foreground; not liquid wastewater, humidity uptake, steam purchased as Product, heavy/tritiated water or an aggregate VOC/COD indicator. Match a more specific real air compartment when known.

- Selected flow: water vapour `fe0acd60-3ddc-11dd-ac04-0050c2490048`

### Process: Complete-toy inspection and first release (`release`)

#### Inputs

#### Outputs

##### Product flows

###### First-released complete toy tricycle (`finished`)

Only the unique first complete quality release. Q is measured complete net mass summed over individually accepted configurations, including first-released closing FG once. All integral components and accepted integral items detached solely for transport are included once with measured reconciliation; packaging, loose tools, spares, rider and payload are excluded. This is an actual whole-release gate, not a declaration that a source model is shipped fully assembled. A boxed part kit, reinspection, shipment or repaired previously released toy never creates Q.

Native Product flow: finished manufactured product; production mix at plant. Its commentary positively includes children's tricycles and excludes bicycles, while also covering other wheeled toys and dolls' carriages. Use here only for the complete non-electric direct-front-wheel-pedal tricycle in §1; never a frame, wheel, kit, adult/cargo cycle, pushchair, scooter or electric toy. No fixed material, grade, geography, lifetime or single-item mass is supplied. Mass/kg is the reference property; no secondary-property conversion or inferred mass is used.

- Selected flow: Dolls' carriages, wheeled toys designed to be ridden by children `5111519e-552a-4501-b70c-2f955b0a2217`
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg
- Amount rule: 1 kg
- Value mode: Calculated value (`calculated_value`)
- Specificity: Site-specific (`site_specific`)
- Normalization basis: per 1 kg reference flow
- Basis kind: Reference flow (`reference_flow`)
- Evidence kind: Calculated from collection (`calculated_from_collection`)
- Collection protocol: `cp_release`
- Sources: `winther-toy-catalogue-2025`; `winther-451-assembly`; `winther-451-spares`; `kriddo-factory-assembly-checks`

###### Warehouse and actual service electricity (`service_electricity`)

Actual incremental packing, storage, handling, inspection, permitted or unauthorized post-release work, hold and disposition energy, traced to original Qc and observation horizon H. No unrelated current manufacturing denominator.

Native Product flow is <1 kV alternating current, consumption mix delivered to the user, grid-average supply with mixed generation. Use only for a compatible measured user-side supply and matching geographic/time provider; it is not dedicated hydropower, on-site generation, medium-voltage power or fuel. Native extensive reference is Net calorific value/MJ used here for electrical energy; retain metered kWh with exact conversion 3.6 MJ/kWh, not a heating-value or mass factor.

### Process: Individual gross packaging pools (`packaging`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Corrugated shipping carton (`carton_p`)

Conditional separately purchased new article P for this one corrugated shipping carton. The single-quotient numerator is the complete actual gross physical leg V, before economic shares or stock/burden correction. This disclosure row creates no second purchase, provider burden or credit. P and I are mutually exclusive ownership tags for the same inbound article; a whole wet incoming article includes its constituent water once. Record real influx even when the article/water remains in closing stock. All opening/closing empty, attached-WIP/FG and reusable-pool quantities stay in the pool equation. Only other same-direction incoming/outgoing flow terms can create X deduplication; a stock or opposite row cannot. Internal reuse cancels. Trace its one original upstream owner and any valid burden handover in the separate burden dimension; full physical gross V is never reduced by an allocation share.

One actual complete corrugated shipping carton, including its supplied board, adhesive, print and water once. Declare actual fibre/board construction, coatings, dimensions, wet tare, provider gate and composition independently. It cannot represent a liner, stretch film, tape, wooden article, all packaging or waste. A generic unspecified packaging family does not establish this specific whole article; retain its unbound identity until a compatible complete native record is verified.

- Selected flow: Whole corrugated shipping carton
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg

P and I are mutually exclusive ownership tags for the same inbound article; a whole wet incoming article includes its constituent water once. Record real influx even when the article/water remains in closing stock. All opening/closing empty, attached-WIP/FG and reusable-pool quantities stay in the pool equation. Only other same-direction incoming/outgoing flow terms can create X deduplication; a stock or opposite row cannot. Internal reuse cancels. Trace its one original upstream owner and any valid burden handover in the separate burden dimension; full physical gross V is never reduced by an allocation share.

Native Product flow is polyethylene film, CAS9002-88-4, production mix at plant, Mass/kg; no fixed thickness, grade, route or geography. The commentary distinguishes PET as a competing polyester, not this PE identity. Its luggage/leather classification is an isolated taxonomy defect; PE basename, CAS and physical film description agree. Use for one actual PE stretch-wrap film with verified supplier formulation, never PET, resin pellets, paper tape, a whole carton or a mixed waste. Supplier and measured film properties remain required.

##### Elementary flows

###### Ordinary H2O vapour released to air (`packaging_vapour_out`)

Actual independently measured H2O vapour loss during storage, handling, cleanup or actual operation. Do not force an equal opposite input, treat all water as evaporated or fit two unknowns to one mass residual. An integrated vapour quantity is not multiplied by time again. Constituent water already leaving in a wet article or liquid effluent is excluded from this same-direction vapour term.

Native Elementary flow is H2O water vapour, CAS7732-18-5, emissions to air with unspecified air subcompartment, Mass/kg. The synonym dump contains liquid water, ice and isotopic-water strings; these do not override the vapour basename, CAS and air destination. Use only measured ordinary H2O vapour leaving this foreground; not liquid wastewater, humidity uptake, steam purchased as Product, heavy/tritiated water or an aggregate VOC/COD indicator. Match a more specific real air compartment when known.

- Selected flow: water vapour `fe0acd60-3ddc-11dd-ac04-0050c2490048`

### Process: Durable tools and actual repair/fluid stocks (`assets`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Actual fulfilled electric assembly screwdriver service (`driver_service`)

Only actual observed fulfilled electric assembly screwdriver service of the declared physical configuration and original cohort. Record real use intervals, duty/capacity, existing/new/used asset history and a compatible provider or own-asset burden vector; no fixed lifetime, nominal one-tool-per-kg or current purchase assumption. Full physical acquisition/return/retirement and wet fleet mass remain separate and are never reduced by a service share. Included parts, fluids and maintenance are singly owned. The h unit is used only with actual timed service and a justified compatible provider/capacity conversion; otherwise the result remains unresolved rather than inventing a conversion. For a reused cloth retain its complete substrate/retained material/water history and the actual final whole contaminated outlet. A disposable cloth consumed once is not additionally assigned a duplicated reusable-service burden.

- Selected flow: Actual fulfilled electric assembly screwdriver service
- Flow property / unit: Time / h
- Amount rule: Retrieve the saved normalized_exchange for cp_driver_service; raw collection and this retrieval perform no extra division.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per 1 kg reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_driver_service`
- Sources: `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010`

The h unit is used only with actual timed service and a justified compatible provider/capacity conversion; otherwise the result remains unresolved rather than inventing a conversion. For a reused cloth retain its complete substrate/retained material/water history and the actual final whole contaminated outlet. A disposable cloth consumed once is not additionally assigned a duplicated reusable-service burden.

##### Elementary flows

###### Atmospheric H2O vapour taken up (`assets_humidity_in`)

Conditional measured atmospheric H2O uptake in actual storage/handling or operation, independently from water losses. Keep the full new influx and full wet closing mass simultaneously; subtract X_in only if the same water already belongs to another incoming term. No permission or repair is required for natural humidity exchange. An emission-to-water water-vapour UUID cannot be used for this resource input.

##### Elementary flows

###### Ordinary H2O vapour released to air (`assets_vapour_out`)

Actual independently measured H2O vapour loss during storage, handling, cleanup or actual operation. Do not force an equal opposite input, treat all water as evaporated or fit two unknowns to one mass residual. An integrated vapour quantity is not multiplied by time again. Constituent water already leaving in a wet article or liquid effluent is excluded from this same-direction vapour term.

Native Elementary flow is H2O water vapour, CAS7732-18-5, emissions to air with unspecified air subcompartment, Mass/kg. The synonym dump contains liquid water, ice and isotopic-water strings; these do not override the vapour basename, CAS and air destination. Use only measured ordinary H2O vapour leaving this foreground; not liquid wastewater, humidity uptake, steam purchased as Product, heavy/tritiated water or an aggregate VOC/COD indicator. Match a more specific real air compartment when known.

- Selected flow: water vapour `fe0acd60-3ddc-11dd-ac04-0050c2490048`
- Flow property / unit: Mass `93a60a56-a3c8-11da-a746-0800200b9a66` / kg

### Process: Once-owned actual waste treatment (`treatment`)

##### Product flows

###### Actual treatment of whole contaminated wipe from assembly (`assembly_contaminated_wipe_treatment`)

Use the actual complete provider demand corresponding to the full physical transferred Qt, including substrate, retained material and water. Retain that total once, then distinguish disjoint per-cohort service/burden vectors and their original Q0. A_j is not a shrunken physical receiver transfer. No separate constituent treatment beside the whole provider, no provider aggregate plus duplicate expanded treatment, and no second charge if the wipe belongs to the one mixed outlet. Unknown actual receiver, classification, composition or assignment prevents a dependent treatment result; no assumed zero or credit.

#### Outputs

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a_attributional | all attributable burden dimensions | Use an explicitly attributional goal. First isolate exclusive operations and directly assigned exchanges; subdivide or virtually subdivide shared assembly, testing, handling and asset services wherever real records allow. Then determine exchange-specific physical causality from the operation that actually determines that input/emission/service. A convenient mass share or arbitrary physical/economic choice is not causality. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| a_residual | genuine residual multifunctionality | Only the genuinely inseparable remainder may use common-process-gate market values for the real output states and same observation period, with quantities, prices, geography, sensitivity and reason physical causality cannot resolve it disclosed. Do not impose a fixed share, transport-stage final price, recycled-content credit or avoided-production substitution. No presumed coproduct is created by a failed toy, packaging return, reusable asset, scrap sale or treatment output. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| a_vectors | manufacturing and original-cohort services | Conserve per-exchange inherited vectors across raw/WIP/FG/repair/packaging/asset stores and cohort handoffs. A legitimate other-cohort/function transfer requires physical identity, receiving owner and a justified allocation/handover decision once. Physical outward waste or sale alone never subtracts manufacturing/treatment burdens. Service follows its original Qc and horizon H; do not allocate historical services to unrelated new Q. Physical gross V and packaging six legs remain full before any burden allocation. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

The following protocols collect actual unnormalized event/cohort records. Their aggregation_rule names the final reference basis only: it retrieves the one saved normalized_exchange calculated after reconciliation and allocation, and does not divide raw values. The gross view and assigned LCI burden view are separate non-additive views. Actual additional atomic flows require their own row and protocol before a dataset is usable. No site data, missing occurrence or missing UUID is filled with zero.

### Data Collection Protocols

| protocol_id | process_id | flow_role | record_type | raw_fields | collection_method | unit | frequency | temporal_coverage | site_scope | aggregation_rule | quality_evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cp_release | release | finished | weighing and acceptance | serial; configuration; calibrated complete net mass; measured tare; integral detached-parts mass; acceptance status; first-release time; original cohort; actual count; scale calibration and uncertainty | Calibrated actual complete net weighing and acceptance records; reconcile serial and exact installed/delivered integral parts, actual moisture state and measured tare. Sum uniquely first-released measured masses to Q; count reconciles configuration but supplies no kg-per-item default. | kg | Each individual first release | Full cohort period and documented service horizon | Actual manufacturing site and configuration | per 1 kg reference flow | Scale and tare records; serial acceptance; parts reconciliation; release register |
| cp_frame | assembly | frame | actual unnormalized event and stock records | event and direction; physical identity; material and phase; supplier/module completeness; original unit; gross measured V; original cohort and Qc; time and H; opening/closing physical stocks; full wet composition; per-exchange inherited vectors; source owner; actual activity; uncertainty; provider/treatment receipt; X counterpart same-direction term | Collect unnormalized actual records for this one row; weigh mass or meter the stated extensive property with calibration and independent unit/state conversions. Establish the attributable current manufacturing or original-cohort service numerator only after the disjoint physical/stock/vector reconciliation and justified allocation. For W origins in a mixed outlet, retain independent measured origin values without re-adding them to the mixed total. For held FG retain all performed service; for any actually unauthorized or failed work preserve operations and disposition. ### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| raw_conversion | all raw records | Convert original raw totals to their actual native extensive unit before allocation. Integrate a true rate over its measured intervals once; keep already integrated amounts intact. Mass from volume requires independently measured substance-specific density and state. Electricity kWh to MJ uses exact factor3.6; transport uses real gross payload and distance with declared empty-return convention. | raw totals; actual units; state; density; measured intervals | converted raw totals | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| stock_vectors | manufacturing burden dimensions | For each exchange j: U_j = U_direct_j + B_open_store_j + R_store_j - B_close_store_j. U_direct is directly consumed nonstored expenditure or an already consumption-net record; R_store includes only receipts into the disjoint unused/unassigned non-WIP store. No receipt or vector can be in both U_direct and R_store. Apply the store correction once only to that store partition, excluding WIP, FG, packaging and separately assigned asset service. Do not correct an already consumption-net record again. A_j = opening WIP vector_j + U_j - closing WIP vector_j - legitimate T_other_j. T_other exists only for genuinely other cohort/function with justified transfer once, never ordinary waste/scrap sale alone. Closing FG is absent from the subtraction. Physical gross receipts remain full in their separate reconciliation view. | disjoint raw/WIP and non-WIP vectors; actual consumption status; transfer evidence | attributable manufacturing numerator A_j | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| service_vectors | original-cohort service and asset burden dimensions | Determine actual performed service and evidenced asset-use burden by original cohort c and horizon H with the original Qc. Preserve historical vectors and D_rev from the actual original dispatch without assuming equal current return mass. Add true incremental replacement/repair/testing/treatment burdens once. Carry forward only evidenced unused/unassigned service or charge; no subtraction for performed service merely retained in closing FG. | original cohort Qc; H; asset/service histories; receipts; D_rev | attributable service numerator A_cj | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| gross_legs | every gross disclosure row | Select numerator V as the complete actual physical event leg in its actual extensive unit. Keep V unscaled by economic shares, burden allocation or stock-vector subtraction. Pair its independent upstream/service burden only with its original owner once. P/I overlap and X_in/X_out deduplicate other same-direction flow terms only; full new influx and full wet closing stocks coexist. A mixed-outlet origin does not create a second external row/quantity/provider. | actual measured gross leg V; event/direction; original owner; same-direction counterpart | complete gross numerator V | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| single_quotient | all normalized accounting results | normalized_exchange = A / D exactly once after raw conversion and justified reconciliation/allocation. For manufacturing choose A=A_j and D=Q; for original-cohort service/treatment choose A=A_cj and D=Qc; for a physical gross disclosure choose A=full V and D=its declared original first-release Q or Qc. Those are separate non-additive dimensions. When D is zero or unknown retain real raw quantities and loads and issue no unit result. Save the result with dimension/cohort identity; cards and protocol aggregation retrieve that same result without another division. | A; D; dimension; original cohort; saved numerator evidence | normalized_exchange | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| dq_identity | whole toy and all rows | Physical terminal, child purpose, direct pedal route, component version, material/form/state and native constraints must agree; keep ordinary missing identities visible. | Primary full documents; actual BOM and drawings; exact native identity and unit checks |
| dq_scope | all actual operations | Complete period includes failures, zero output, start/stop, idle, cleanup, samples, actual unapproved/failed work and waste/treatment; no missing measured value becomes zero. | Time logs; meters; disposition; bills and supplier coverage |
| dq_vectors | stocks and services | Preserve every inherited exchange vector and vintage with disjoint raw/WIP/non-WIP/FG/repair/packaging/assets; document one numerator and one denominator for each non-additive dimension. | Physical reconciliation; original receipts; cohort and H; vector audit |
| dq_unknowns | dependent dataset | Retain actual uncertainty, ordinary auxiliary/provider/quantity gaps, missing native localization and unsupported core claims honestly; publication or science/translation approval is not inferred. | Gap register; source applicability; measured uncertainty and acceptance evidence |

## 9. Validation Rules

| rule_id | applies_to | rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| v_identity | reference and each bound row | Verify complete child-toy applicability, native dataset type/part-whole/material/form/route/phase/geography/media, every property and true reference unit, and exact official Chinese display. A broad compatible whole manufactured family may be narrowed by positive primary terminal evidence; hard identity conflicts cannot be waived by taxonomy or name similarity. An unconfirmed core reference is a draft/unusable identity hold. An ordinary auxiliary UUID/provider/quantity gap remains explicit and does not by itself invalidate the demonstrated core. | `winther-toy-catalogue-2025`; `winther-451-spares`; `kriddo-factory-assembly-checks` |
| v_reference | finished; all accounting results | Require first whole acceptance and same-configuration calibrated net weighing. Confirm Q includes released closing FG once, every numerator retains failures/idle/cleanup/testing and appropriate stocks/vectors, and only one saved quotient is used. Q=0 or missing Q preserves raw loads and produces no unit result; no unknown may be called zero or passed. Native MJ, kg, m3 and transport properties remain their own extensive units. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| v_reconciliation | all physical gates | Reconcile manufacture, FG/service and combined physical gates; each wet packaging pool and each gross leg; actual water phases/species; raw/WIP/non-WIP and durable-asset/service vectors. Verify X only against another same-direction flow term and retain wet closing stock plus actual new influx. Verify mixed totals versus origin subaccounts and treatment/provider ownership once. A mass residual is not independent water/species evidence. | `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |
| v_quality | actual dataset production | Retain incoming component version compatibility, actual fastening/rotation/steering/seat checks, safety and destructive-test results, permission/postwork tests and disposition where actual work occurred, whole upstream coverage and uncertainties. Conformity/age/load claims need their actual market-specific evidence; no source certificate is transferred. A mechanical/schema/projection check does not constitute scientific review, translation approval, mapping acceptance or publication. | `winther-451-assembly`; `winther-451-spares`; `kriddo-factory-assembly-checks`; `ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010` |

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | secondary_dataset; background_dataset |
| downstream_use | Configured attributional first-release manufacturing dataset plus separately identified actual original-cohort service horizon |
| allowed_use | Compatible complete non-electric direct-pedal child toy supply with verified whole upstream coverage and declared components/gates; scope-specific comparison only |
| excluded_use | Exclude adult, passenger/cargo or road-transport tricycles; bicycles and balance bikes; scooters; electric or combustion toys; hand-lever and solely push-propelled ride-ons; dolls' carriages and strollers; multi-rider/recumbent variants; toy train/model/construction sets; loose wheels, raw steel/plastic, component-only kits and remanufacturing as the reference terminal. Riding, child metabolism, road infrastructure, use-stage repairs and end of life are not part of the manufacturing reference. Actual pre-dispatch storage/handling/packing and actual returned-product work within the declared service horizon remain separately traceable to original release cohorts. |
| required_metadata | actual manufacturer and model; serial and first-release cohort; child-toy purpose and direct front-wheel pedal drive; frame substrate grade and welded geometry; actual coating and supplied frame completeness; fork and handlebar module version; seat rubber specification and fitting; solid tyre and hub specification; steel crank and non-slip pedal configuration; wheel bearing and head-tube bush identities by version; grips and steering-stop configuration; axle fasteners and actual component supply overlaps; all integral delivered accessories; calibrated net whole-toy weighing and tare; configuration-specific acceptance plan and results; actual rework permission and postwork disposition; site geography and production period; utility state and upstream supplier coverage; opening and closing raw WIP and unassigned-store vectors; released FG and original-cohort service horizon; gross dispatch returns and original-dispatch reversal; each packaging pool and supplier-included ownership; allocation evidence and unresolved actual quantities |
| required_quality_disclosure | Primary-source limitations; component version and actual acceptance; supplier coverage; native restrictions; unresolved identities/quantities; allocation and all cohort/stock/service/physical-gross reconciliation; observed H and unobserved future |
| update_trigger | Actual route, component/BOM, material/finish, release gate, source version, supply geography, native identity, provider, allocation or service horizon changes require review in this same single record |

| Source id | Type | Reference | Used for |

| winther-toy-catalogue-2025 | handbook | Winther Catalogue 2025–2026, complete manufacturer-authored PDF, pp11 and15–17; https://www.alprovi.nl/uploads/documents/winther_catalogue_2025.pdf | Complete child tricycle terminal; rubber seat/grips/stops and solid rubber tyres; industrial ball-bearing version; steel crank; upstream weld/powder-coat illustration. Distributor-hosted original, not same-factory procurement or quantitative defaults. |

Winther A/S, 451 Assembly instruction and instructions for use, complete2-page original; https://www.a-winther.com/Files/Filer/winther/Assembly-Instructions/451_Assembly.pdf | Actual handlebar/fork fitment and fastening; component maintenance and compatibility limitations. Adult/user assembly instructions do not alone prove factory manufacture or that shipment was fully assembled; no age/load/certification transfer. |
| winther-451-spares | handbook | A. Winther A/S, 451 Spare Parts, complete2-page exploded drawing and451.00 version2.1 table; https://www.a-winther.com/Files/Filer/winther/Spare-Parts/451_Spares.pdf | Whole supplied fork/grip/fitting and front-wheel/crank/pedal modules; rear wheel/axle/seat and small-part relationships; disjoint supply-completeness protocol. No material recipe or universal component version. |
| kriddo-factory-assembly-checks | literature | KRIDDO, Inside KRIDDO’s Factory: Where Safety and Responsibility Come to Life, full manufacturer HTML; https://kriddo.com/blogs/brand-story/inside-kriddo-factory-where-safety-and-responsibility-come-to-life | Explicit tricycle component selection/compatibility and safety checks before factory exit as a qualitative manufacturing/release example. Adjacent balance-bike welding passages and duplicated marketing claims do not establish this toy’s raw fabrication or quantitative/certification defaults. |
| ilcd-attributional-allocation-2010 | official_guidance | European Commission JRC, ILCD Handbook General guide for Life Cycle Assessment—Detailed guidance, first edition March2010, §7.9.2–7.9.3, printed pp255–272; https://eplca.jrc.ec.europa.eu/uploads/ILCD-Handbook-General-guide-for-LCA-DETAILED-GUIDANCE-12March2010-ISBN-fin-v1.0-EN.pdf | Attributional subdivision/virtual subdivision, exchange-specific determining physical causality and genuine residual market-value allocation; consistent boundary, cohort and evidence-based bookkeeping requirements are this PCR’s explicit implementation. Not toy industrial manufacturing or numerical allocation evidence. |
| un-cpc3-toys | official_guidance | UNSD CPC3.0 Explanatory Notes30June2025, complete official original,pp191–193 and bicycle exclusion49921; https://unstats.un.org/unsd/classifications/Econ/Download/In%20Text/CPC_Ver_3.0_Exp_Notes_30Jun2025.pdf | Explicit child-tricycle inclusion and bicycle exclusion; adjacent dolls/parts/scale-model categories. Classification context only; no material recipe, manufacturing evidence or entire-leaf methodology approval. |
